- **rfauto-branchline-coupler**: 用 rfauto 设计、仿真或调参 branchline coupler（90° hybrid）时使用。内容包括 λ/4 臂阻抗综合，以及配方驱动的 run、tune、autotune、calibrate 工作流。触发词：branchline、branch-line、hybrid、90 degree coupler、quadrature、分支线电桥、90度电桥、正交电桥。ratrace（180°）与 Lange、gysel 耦合器不适用本卡，见其他 rfauto 卡。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 下的卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-branchline-coupler) · ★157)
- **rfauto-cpw**: 涉及 rfauto 的 `cpw` 卡时使用：均匀共面波导（锚族，中心带加两侧地，地延伸到域边；CPWPort 为一等端口，按 gap_width 口径）。可调参数：w_mm、gap_mm、line_len_mm。指标提取：CPWPort 1-2 处的 S11、S21，其中 S21 的相位斜率给出 εeff。触发词：cpw。其他卡族不适用，请改用对应的 rfauto 卡或 skill。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 下的卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-cpw) · ★157)

- **rfauto-mline-synthesis**: 需要在给定基板上求微带线（mline）的线宽或阻抗时使用。例如：Rogers 4350B 0.508 mm 上的 50 欧线宽，1.1 mm 线在 2.4 GHz 下的 Z0，这条微带的 epsilon_eff；或者求 λ/4 臂长。Wilkinson、branchline、patch 馈线需要 50、70.7、35.35 欧线宽时也用。用户贴来一段 openEMS 或 HFSS 的 mline run，问 |S11| 为何非零，或怎样从 S21 相位斜率读出 epsilon_eff，同样适用。触发词：mline、microstrip、line width、impedance、Z0、epsilon_eff、stackup、微带线、线宽、阻抗、等效介电常数、λ/4 臂长、层叠。CPW、CPS、stripline、SIW（同族的 rfauto 卡）、ADS LineCalc 图形界面问题和 PCB 载流能力问题不适用本卡。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 下的卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-mline-synthesis) · ★157)
- **rfauto-patch-antenna**: 用 rfauto 设计、仿真或调参矩形微带贴片天线时使用。涵盖腔模型谐振、贴片宽度与长度综合、馈电内缩（inset feed），以及配方驱动的 run、tune、autotune、calibrate 工作流。触发词：patch antenna、microstrip antenna、patch width、inset feed、resonance frequency、贴片天线、微带天线、谐振频率、馈电。PIFA、IFA 和贴片阵列（见其他 rfauto 卡），以及一般性 PCB 天线问题，不适用本卡。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 下的卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-patch-antenna) · ★157)
- **rfauto-pifa**: 涉及 rfauto 的 `pifa` 卡时使用：PIFA（§10.3 C1）。结构为贴片位于 z=h，尺寸 L×W；+x 边的短路板宽 Ws，从 z=0 触地到 z=h；馈针距短路板 pin_back，y 向偏移 pin_y，针顶触贴片、针底触地。LumpedPort 沿针的 z 向放置，基板加 z-min 处的 PEC 构成无限大地。可调参数：pifa_l_mm、pifa_w_mm、pifa_ws_mm、pin_back_mm、pin_y_mm。指标提取：LumpedPort 1 处的 S11（PIFA 居中短路板 λ/4：谷位、谷深）。L 经路径式定版的标称值为 17.0785，真机 override 取 17.08，在 2.26GHz 处为 −8.79dB（−5.8%），PASS；谷点 Zin=105−14jΩ，详见 smoke_note。触发词：pifa。其他卡族不适用，请改用对应的 rfauto 卡或 skill。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-pifa) · ★157)
- **rfauto-wilkinson-power-divider**: 用 rfauto 设计、仿真或调参 Wilkinson 功分器时使用。涵盖两路等分、λ/4 臂综合和隔离电阻，以及配方驱动的 run、tune、autotune、calibrate 工作流。触发词：wilkinson、power divider、splitter、isolation resistor、arm length、威尔金森、功分器、等分、隔离电阻、臂长。N 路和不等分变体（见其他 rfauto 卡），以及 branchline、ratrace、gysel 耦合器，不适用本卡。数值只来自 rfauto 内核与 references/ 下的卡片文件。 ([zai-org/zcode-plugins](https://github.com/zai-org/zcode-plugins/tree/HEAD/external_plugins/rfauto/skills/rfauto-wilkinson-power-divider) · ★157)

- **rfc9000-handshake-done-timing**: Audit QUIC server handling of HANDSHAKE_DONE frame send-timing and receipt processing per RFC 9000 §19.20 and RFC 9001 §4.9.2 — timing and key-discard-order errors can be triggered by a malicious server/peer into assertion crashes (related CVE-2025-59530 quic-go, observed in the wild) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9000-handshake-done-timing))
- **rfc9000-migration-congestion-window**: Audit whether QUIC implementations violate the RFC 9000 §8.1 anti-amplification 3x limit and §9.4 pre-validation rate-limiting obligations during connection migration / NAT rebinding — sending at full speed to an unvalidated address opens a UDP reflection-amplification and traffic-hijacking attack surface (related CVE-2026-35191 OpenSSL QUIC non-validating-mode accounting error violating 3x; TMA 2025: 20% of IPv4 servers violate the amplification limit; HAProxy#3329 disable_active_migration over-interpretation empirical finding) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9000-migration-congestion-window))
- **rfc9000-new-token-replay**: Audit QUIC implementations' protection of NEW_TOKEN frames and cross-connection token replay per RFC 9000 §19.7/§8.1.4 — single-use constraints, the IP-binding MUST, and unlimited replay would let the address-validation system be bypassed (blind-spot clause; format custom + scattered MUSTs = high-variance surface) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9000-new-token-replay))

- **rfc9000-stream-reset-cancel**: Audit whether QUIC stream reset/cancel (RESET_STREAM/STOP_SENDING) state-machine validation and cost symmetry comply with RFC 9000 §3.4/§3.5 — Final Size invariants, missing STREAM_STATE_ERROR on state mismatch, and cancellation cost far below server processing cost (H3 Rapid Reset DoS surface; related CVE-2023-44487 same-pattern in HTTP/2, H3 stacks advised to mitigate proactively; 2025 MadeYouReset variant proves the family keeps evolving) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9000-stream-reset-cancel))

- **rfc9113-h2c-upgrade-pseudoheader**: Audit h2c upgrade negotiation and pseudo-header validation — a reverse proxy forwarding Upgrade: h2c + HTTP2-Settings opens a raw-byte tunnel that bypasses authentication/WAF (CVE-2026-88008 Traefik GHSA-w4v4-9rw7-5326 CVSS 9.1 CWE-444+863, fixed in PR #13797 stripping the h2c token; CVE-2026-88011 v2 sibling; CVE-2026-73548 Envoy generic-upgrade smuggling); HTTP2-Settings must not be forwarded with requests (RFC 9113 §3.2), and pseudo-header rules (§8.3) are the audit anchors; BishopFox h2csmuggler demonstrated default forwarding in HAProxy/Traefik/Nuster as early as 2020 ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-h2c-upgrade-pseudoheader))
- **rfc9113-ping-empty-frame-flood**: Audit whether HTTP/2 implementations bound the processing cost of mandatory-response frames (PING) and empty frames (DATA/HEADERS/CONTINUATION/PUSH_PROMISE) — PING forces a per-frame ACK (CVE-2019-9512 Ping Flood: sustained PING inflates the response queue, exhausting CPU/memory); empty frames bypass flow control with asymmetric per-frame cost (CVE-2019-9518 Empty Frame Flood, disclosed with Envoy security-team acknowledgment); zero-length header memory residency (CVE-2019-9516 Header Leak); RFC 9113 §6.7/§6.1 give the semantics but no cost guardrails ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-ping-empty-frame-flood))
- **rfc9113-priority-tree-abuse**: Audit whether HTTP/2 priority-tree implementations resist resource exhaustion via cycles — continuously reshuffling priorities across many streams churns the tree structure and exhausts CPU (CVE-2019-9513 Resource Loop: nghttp2/nginx/netty/undertow full-stack impact, CVSS 7.5, fixed in nghttp2 1.39.2); RFC 9113 §5.3 permits implementations to deprioritize processing (blank-clause escape), but "if you keep it, it must resist churn" — the audit target is the boundedness of the priority state machine ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-priority-tree-abuse))
- **rfc9113-rapid-reset-concurrency**: Audit HTTP/2 implementations' concurrency-limit accounting and forced-close obligations per RFC 9113 §5.1.2/§6.4 — only open/half-closed streams count toward concurrency (closed releases the slot immediately), RST_STREAM closes without acknowledgment so "open then cancel" consumes no concurrency slot; without a connection-level work budget this is the original CVE-2023-44487 Rapid Reset vulnerability surface (Google/AWS/Cloudflare all affected, one of the largest L7 DDoS events in history, 2023-10; variants: batched delayed cancel, over-limit stream opens relying on per-stream rejection) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-rapid-reset-concurrency))
- **rfc9113-server-push-lifecycle**: Audit HTTP/2 server-push (PUSH_PROMISE / reserved-stream) lifecycle implementations — enabling push is an attack-surface amplifier: Cache-Digest header triggering a crash on push (CVE-2020-9490 Apache httpd 2.4.20-2.4.43, CVSS 7.5, mitigated by H2Push off); push-method access-control failure (CVE-2023-30631 Apache Traffic Server push_method_enabled config ineffective, CVSS 7.5); http4s Ember push-frame DoS (CVE-2026-69203, mitigated by proxy stripping PUSH_PROMISE); RFC 9113 §6.6/§8.4 require that clients can reject push and servers can advertise ENABLE_PUSH=0, but reserved-stream state-machi… ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-server-push-lifecycle))
- **rfc9113-settings-frame-flood**: Audit whether HTTP/2 implementations bound SETTINGS-frame processing — repeatedly sending SETTINGS frames with an excessive entry count exhausts CPU (CVE-2020-11080 nghttp2 &lt;1.41.0: a 14,400-byte frame with 2,400 entries repeated saturates the CPU, impacting the entire ecosystem including Node.js/Oracle MySQL); the RFC only requires the length-multiple-of-6 check and in-order processing — an entry-count cap is the implementation's obligation (blind-spot clause) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-settings-frame-flood))
- **rfc9113-stream-state-machine**: Audit HTTP/2 stream-state-machine handling of illegal frame transitions — DATA on a half-closed(remote) stream and similar violations must be answered with STREAM_CLOSED, and manufacturing them in bulk becomes the MadeYouReset resource drain (CVE-2025-5115 Jetty GHSA-mmxm-8w33-wc4h); the HEADERS+RST_STREAM race with double callbacks and double cleanup can cause double-free (Apache h2_mplx.c, disclosed 2026); the RST_STREAM refused-stream path forcing an in-scope write can cause heap UAF (CVE-2026-56848 Node.js, fixed in commit ba6cb5c); sustained RST floods leaking memory on connection close… ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http2/rfc9113-stream-state-machine))
- **rfc9114-content-length-data-mismatch**: Audit whether HTTP/3 targets violate RFC 9114 §4.1.2 — the Content-Length value must equal the sum of received DATA frame lengths; a violation that gets forwarded implies an H3→H1 request-smuggling surface (related CVE-2026-33555 HAProxy) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-content-length-data-mismatch))
- **rfc9114-goaway-drain-enforcement**: Audit whether HTTP/3 implementations enforce GOAWAY graceful-shutdown semantics strictly per RFC 9114 §5.2/§7.2.6 — identifier monotonicity, rejecting new requests after receipt, and the conservative default on GOAWAY-less shutdown (evidence: quiche#2700, quic-go#5326, erlang_quic 1.0.0 fix; no standalone CVE) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-goaway-drain-enforcement))
- **rfc9114-grease-reserved-frames**: Audit whether HTTP/3 implementations meet the tolerance obligations for GREASE/reserved frame types, reserved SETTINGS identifiers, and unknown unidirectional stream types per RFC 9114 §9/§7.2.8 — unknown frames must be length-skipped, never rejected; H2 reserved frames 0x02/0x06/0x08/0x09 must kill the connection, never be GREASE-skipped; rejecting GREASE is a forward-compatibility break (evidence: aioquic &lt;1.6 stream-tail GREASE frame caused stream-data loss; confusing reserved frames with H2 smuggling frames opens a smuggling backdoor) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-grease-reserved-frames))
- **rfc9114-pseudo-header-field-validation**: Audit whether HTTP/3 targets handle malformed pseudo-headers/fields strictly per RFC 9114 §4.1.2/§4.2/§4.3 malformed judgments — lenient handling implies a parsing-split and H3→H1 smuggling attack surface (related nginx#1043 unfixed; H2 historical pattern replicated) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-pseudo-header-field-validation))
- **rfc9114-qpack-decoded-size-limit**: Audit whether HTTP/3 targets limit only the compressed QPACK frame length but not the decoded field-section size — no decoded-size limit means a compression-amplification memory-exhaustion attack surface (thousands-fold amplification per connection possible; related CVE-2025-64702/CVE-2026-40898 quic-go, CVE-2026-12523 quiche, CVE-2025-13945 Wireshark) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-qpack-decoded-size-limit))
- **rfc9114-server-push-frame-rejection**: Audit whether HTTP/3 clients/servers strictly reject server-push legacy frames — PUSH_PROMISE and MAX_PUSH_ID receipt must terminate the connection with H3_FRAME_UNEXPECTED (RFC 9114 §7.2.5/§7.2.7); silently swallowing them means a missing protocol-error obligation (evidence: quic-go#5686, inconsistent handling within the same function in v0.60.0) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-server-push-frame-rejection))
- **rfc9114-settings-frame-order**: Audit whether HTTP/3 implementations enforce SETTINGS-frame strictness per RFC 9114 §7.2.1 — SETTINGS must be the first frame on each control stream, and a duplicate SETTINGS must be treated as an H3_FRAME_UNEXPECTED connection error (blind-spot clause, no public CVE yet) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-settings-frame-order))
- **rfc9114-trailers-frame-sequence**: Audit whether HTTP/3 implementations handle trailer and multi-HEADERS frame sequences per RFC 9114 §4.1/§4.2 — DATA after trailing HEADERS is a connection error, trailer vs Content-Length consistency, missing validation of duplicate response header sections is an H3-layer message-smuggling surface (blind-spot clause; nginx#1043 same ecosystem; H2 trailer/CL historical smuggling pattern replicated) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9114-trailers-frame-sequence))
- **rfc9204-qpack-dynamic-table-capacity**: Audit whether QPACK dynamic-table capacity negotiation and size-update ordering strictly follow RFC 9204 §3.2 — over-capacity insertion and out-of-order size updates are encoder/decoder stream errors, a compression-layer state-machine attack surface of the same family as CVE-2025-64702 ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9204-qpack-dynamic-table-capacity))
- **rfc9218-priority-update-validation**: Audit whether HTTP/3 implementations' validation of the PRIORITY_UPDATE frame (RFC 9218 §7.2) is complete — frame position, target stream type, out-of-limit stream IDs, push-stream references, and priority field-value parse failures are all connection-error obligations whose absence is a blind-spot clause (erlang_quic Strict PRIORITY_UPDATE parsing fix as evidence) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9218-priority-update-validation))
- **rfc9297-capsule-content-length**: Audit whether HTTP Datagrams / Capsule Protocol implementations strictly follow RFC 9297 §3.2/§3.3/§3.5 — capsule coexisting with Content-Length and other headers, truncated capsules, and unknown capsule-type handling errors are an HTTP-smuggling same-family attack surface (same family: an undisclosed sharing-session smuggling case, CVE-2025-64702 pattern) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9297-capsule-content-length))
- **rfc9368-version-negotiation-validation**: Audit whether QUIC implementations handle version negotiation and downgrade protection per RFC 8999 §6 and RFC 9368 §4 — Version Negotiation packet invariants and the full set of Version Information validation MUSTs; their absence is a version-downgrade and parse-crash attack surface (evidence: QUIC-FUZZ xquic VN-event crash; CVE-2024-45396 Quicly same-family pattern) ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/rfc-audit-skills/http3/rfc9368-version-negotiation-validation))

- **rfdb-metadata-gotchas**: Fix silent metadata issues in RFDB node storage. Covers two traps: (1) metadata flattening — nested metadata fields become top-level after serialization, so node.metadata.field is undefined but node.field works. (2) reserved keys — fields named "type", "id", "name", "file", "exported" are silently stripped from metadata by _parseNode() to prevent overwriting top-level node fields. ([Disentinel/grafema](https://github.com/Disentinel/grafema/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grafema-metadata-flattening) · ★35)
- **rfdb-stale-node-cleanup**: Recover from stale RFDB nodes that survive `commit_batch` cleanup despite having their `file` field listed in `changed_files`. Use when: (1) re-running an enricher/orchestrator step but old data with the same file field stays in the graph, (2) `commit_batch(changed_files=[X], ...)` reports success but a query later still returns nodes with `file = X`, (3) you re-pointed structural nodes (e.g., DIRECTORY/FILE) from a synthetic file path to real file paths and the old synthetic-path nodes won't go away, (4) graph-stream / find_by_type returns a mix of "stale" and "new" nodes for the same logica… ([Disentinel/grafema](https://github.com/Disentinel/grafema/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rfdb-stale-node-cleanup) · ★35)
- **rfdb-v2-clear-ephemeral-trap**: Fix RFDB V2 graph data silently not persisting to disk after analysis. Use when: (1) rfdb-server reports "0 nodes, 0 edges" on restart despite successful analysis, (2) segment directories exist but are empty (no .bin files), (3) manifest_index.json shows total_nodes: 0 despite analysis logging 70k+ nodes, (4) Docker builds produce empty graph databases, (5) --clear flag used before analyze command. Root cause: GraphEngineV2::clear() replaces the store with MultiShardStore::ephemeral() which has path: None, causing all flush operations to write to in-memory buffers only. ([Disentinel/grafema](https://github.com/Disentinel/grafema/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rfdb-v2-clear-ephemeral-trap) · ★35)
- **rfdb-write-lock-edge-flush-gotcha**: RFDB server becomes unresponsive (60s+ timeouts) during enricher addEdges write storms. Use when: (1) RFDB CPU spikes to 60-90% after addEdges calls; (2) subsequent reads time out at 60s; (3) server stays catatonic for 5-15 min after a write storm; (4) Tokio main thread parked (write-lock contention). Root cause: add_edges() calls maybe_auto_flush() which has a memory-pressure path that fires on NODE count — not edge count — and holds the exclusive write lock during disk I/O. Fix: separate maybe_auto_flush_edges() uses byte-limit only. ([Disentinel/grafema](https://github.com/Disentinel/grafema/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rfdb-write-lock-edge-flush-gotcha) · ★35)
